Ingredients                       Directions
2/3 cup sugar                     Mix sugar, corn starch and salt in a large microwaveable bowl.
2 tablespoons Argo® Corn Starch   Whisk in milk and egg yolks until well blended.
1/4 teaspoon salt
2 cups milk                       Microwave on HIGH (100%) for about 5 to 7 minutes, stirring every

Vanilla Extract
                                  Chill 30 minutes.

                           Capirotada (Mexican Bread Pudding)
Ingredients                       Directions
1 (1 pound) loaf white bread      Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
2 tablespoons butter
1 cup raisins                     Combine water, cinnamon, and sugar in a medium saucepan. Bring
1 cup pineapple chunks, drained   to a boil and let simmer for about 15 minutes. Set aside.
4 ounces Colby longhorn cheese
3/4 cup chopped walnuts           Toast bread and butter each slice on one side. Arrange toast in a
                                  single layer in a large casserole dish. Sprinkle bread with raisins,
10 cinnamon sticks
                                  nuts, and pineapple. Slice cheese and place over this mixture.
2 cups white sugar
                                  Repeat layers until all bread is used, making sure enough cheese is
2 cups water
                                  left over for the top. Pour the cinnamon syrup mixture over
                                  everything in baking dish.

                                  Bake for 30 minutes. Remove from oven and cool for at least 15
                                  minutes.

                                                  Chocolate Bread Pudding
Ingredients                       Directions
PUDDING                           Preheat oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C). Grease a 9x13
1 (8 ounce) loaf baguette         baking dish.
1/2 cup unsalted butter, melted
1 cup semisweet chocolate chips   Slice baguette in 1/4 inch slices and brush one side with melted
3 cups heavy cream                butter. Place on a baking sheet and into preheated oven until
1 cup milk                        golden, about 1 minute.
2 eggs
                                  Melt chocolate chips in microwave or double boiler. Set aside.
8 egg yolks, room temperature
1/2 cup white sugar               In a medium saucepan over low heat, heat cream and milk until
1 tablespoon vanilla extract      warm. Do not scald.
SAUCE
2 ounces unsweetened chocolate,   In a large bowl beat eggs, egg yolks, 1/2 cup sugar and 1
chopped                           tablespoon vanilla until smooth. Blend in warm cream mixture a
1 tablespoon butter               little at a time. Whisk egg mixture into melted chocolate until
1/3 cup boiling water             smooth.
1/2 cup white sugar
3 tablespoons corn syrup          Arrange toasted bread slices, buttered side up, in prepared dish
1 teaspoon vanilla extract        until dish is full. Pour chocolate mixture over bread and let rest 40
                                  minutes, until liquid is absorbed.

                                  Line a roasting pan with a damp kitchen towel. Place baking dish
                                  on towel, inside roasting pan, and place roasting pan on oven rack.
                                  Fill roasting pan with boiling water to reach halfway up the sides of
                                  the baking dish. Bake 40 to 50 minutes, until knife inserted in
                                  center comes out clean. Let rest 8 hours or overnight.

                                  To make sauce, melt unsweetened chocolate in small saucepan
                                  over low heat. Stir in 1 tablespoon butter until melted. Stir in
                                  boiling water, 1/2 cup sugar and corn syrup until smooth. Increase
                                  heat to medium and bring to a boil Boil gently 3 minutes. Remove
                                  from heat and stir in 1 teaspoon vanilla.

                                  To serve: Warm bottom of baking dish, loosen edges of pudding
                                  with a knife then invert cold pudding onto a serving platter. Serve
                                  with warm chocolate sauce.

Sweetener, Granulated             SPLENDA® Granulated Sweetener, eggs, milk, vanilla and

Ingredients                          Directions
1 (5 pound) bone-in beef rib roast   Place the roast bone-side down in a roasting pan, and use a sharp
8 cloves garlic, quartered           paring knife to poke 24 small holes 1 inch deep all over the roast.
3/4 teaspoon salt                    Stuff each hole with a sliver of garlic. Sprinkle with 3/4 teaspoon
1 teaspoon freshly ground black      salt, and LOTS of black pepper. Allow the roast to stand at room
pepper                               temperature for 2 hours. This will take the chill off and allow the
                                     meat to cook more evenly.
2 cups eggs
                                     Meanwhile, beat together the eggs and milk with a pinch of salt until
2 cups milk
                                     smooth. Stir in flour until smooth. Cover the Yorkshire pudding
1 pinch salt
                                     batter, and place in the refrigerator.
2 cups all-purpose flour
1/2 cup pan drippings from prime     Preheat oven to 450 degrees F (230 degrees C).
rib
                                     Cook the roast in the preheated oven for 30 minutes, then lower the
1/2 cup white wine                   heat to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C), and continue roasting until a
2 cups beef stock                    meat thermometer inserted in the center of the meat reaches 140
                                     degrees F (60 degrees C), about 1 hour.

                                     When the roast is done, remove it from the roasting pan, cover with
                                     aluminum foil, and let rest in a warm spot while you make the
                                     Yorkshire pudding. Measure out 1/2 cup of the beef fat, and divide
                                     among 12 muffin tins; pour off the remaining fat, but save the
                                     roasting pan to make the jus.

                                     Place the grease-filled muffin tins into the oven, and turn the
                                     temperature up to 425 degrees F (220 degrees C). Let the muffin
                                     tins preheat for 10 minutes, then fill 3/4 full with the refrigerated
                                     batter, and return to the oven. Bake 20 to 25 minutes until the
                                     puddings have risen tall and are golden brown.

                                     While the Yorkshire puddings are baking, place the roasting pan on
                                     the stove over medium-high heat. Stir in the wine, and bring to a
                                     simmer while scraping to dissolve the pan drippings; pour in the
                                     beef stock and return to a simmer. Cook 10 minutes, then strain and
                                     serve with the roast and puddings.

                                                  Karrie's Yorkshire Pudding
Ingredients                          Directions
3 tablespoons shortening            Preheat oven to 450 degrees F (230 degrees C). Spread shortening
1 1/4 cups all-purpose flour        into a 9x13 inch baking pan and place on center rack in oven.
2 cups low-fat milk
4 eggs                              In a medium bowl, mix flour and 1 cup milk until a smooth paste is
                                    formed. Whisk in eggs one at a time, mixing thoroughly after each
                                    addition. Whisk in remaining 1 cup milk.

                                    As soon as shortening in oven has begun to smoke, remove pan
                                    from oven and pour in batter. Bake in preheated oven for 20
                                    minutes, until golden brown and fluffy. Remove and allow to sit 5
                                    minutes. Slice and serve immediately.
